1</span><h2 class="rb-ch__title">Start here</h2><p class="rb-ch__about">33 rules in 5 groups, explained simply.</p></div></header><h3 class="rb-h">How long is a ban?</h3><p class="rb-p">For the rules in <b>a</b>, <b>b</b> and <b>c</b>, every new ban is longer than the last one:</p><div class="rb-ladder"><div class="rb-step rb-step--0"><span class="rb-step__n">1st ban</span><span class="rb-step__len">3 days</span></div><span class="rb-step__arrow" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" 0 1.65 0l8.58-3.9A1 1 0 0 0 22 17"/></svg></span><b>Many at once = one ban</b><span>For multiple bannable actions around the same time, you will only be punished for one.</span></div><div class="rb-fact"><span class="rb-fact__icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide 6"/></svg></span><b>d and e = permanent now</b><span>Bans under <b>d</b> &amp; <b>e</b> are immediately permanent (<b>e5</b> is an exception which is always 1 Year).</span></div><div class="rb-fact"><span class="rb-fact__icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide your accounts</b><span>Bans are shared between all accounts you own.</span></div></div><h3 class="rb-h">The five groups of rules</h3><p class="rb-p">Every rule has a <b>ban code</b>: its group letter and a number, like <b>c1</b>. The code is written in your ban message.</p><div class="rb-gtiles"><a class="rb-gtile rb-gtile--a" href="#rb-a"><span class="rb-letter">a</span><span class="rb-gtile__t"><b>Behaviour and content</b><small>Things you say, play, wear or do in the game.</small></span><span class="rb-gtile__k rb-gtile__k--strike">Strike</span></a><a class="rb-gtile rb-gtile--b" href="#rb-b"><span class="rb-letter">b</span><span class="rb-gtile__t"><b>Hate and harm</b><small>Slurs, hate, discrimination and wishing harm on people.</small></span><span class="rb-gtile__k rb-gtile__k--strike">Strike</span></a><a class="rb-gtile rb-gtile--c" href="#rb-c"><span class="rb-letter">c</span><span class="rb-gtile__t"><b>Cheating, spam and fake staff</b><small>Pretending to be staff, cheating, exploits, spam and false reports.</small></span><span class="rb-gtile__k rb-gtile__k--strike">Strike</span></a><a class="rb-gtile rb-gtile--d" href="#rb-d"><span class="rb-letter">d</span><span class="rb-gtile__t"><b>Permanent bans</b><small>The most serious rules. Breaking one is a permanent ban straight away.</small></span><span class="rb-gtile__k rb-gtile__k--perm">Permanent</span></a><a class="rb-gtile rb-gtile--e" href="#rb-e"><span class="rb-letter">e</span><span class="rb-gtile__t"><b>Account bans</b><small>Bans for your account itself: names, scams, alts, age and self-bans.</small></span><span class="rb-gtile__k rb-gtile__k--perm">Permanent</span></a></div><h3 class="rb-h">Words used in the rules</h3><p class="rb-p">When a rule uses one of these words, it counts in all of these places. stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-gavel" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m14 13-8.381 8.38a1 1 0 0 1-3.001-3l8.384-8.381M16 16l6-6M21.5 10.5l-8-8M8 8l6-6M8.5 7.5l8 8"/></svg><span>Strike ban: <b>3 days → 7 days → 30 days → permanent</b>. Breaking this same rule twice is permanent.</span></span><button type="button" class="rb-copy" data-code="a2"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-link" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.54.54l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7.07-7.07l-1.72 1.71"/><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 7.07l1.71-1.71"/></svg><span>Copy link</span></button></div></div></div></details><details class="rb-rule rb-rule--a" id="a3" data-search="a3 simulating nsfw actions using animations  **w+s&#x27;ing** means quickly walking forwards and backwards (the w and s keys) against another player. simulating nsfw actions by animating your body  dancing around a pole, pretending to be a stripper on a stripper pole. 2 people using emotes to perform sexual acts together.   using an emote to perform a sexual act on someone, with clear intent that it&#x27;s being used in a sexual way.   w+s&#x27;ing another player who is using an emote in a sexual way, even if you aren&#x27;t doing any emote yourself.   w+s&#x27;ing another player, unless you use an animation or play audios that hint you&#x27;re doing an explicit action w+s&#x27;ing on its own, without those, is too tame to warrant a ban.  using an emote that can be used as a sexual emote, but not near any player emotes on their own can be normal dances. they only become bannable when used in a sexual way with another player.  being afk in an emote that can be used sexually, when a player walks up and performs sexual acts on you you weren&#x27;t actively taking part, so only the player who walked up to you is banned. "><summary><span class="rb-code">a3</span><span class="rb-rule__main"><span class="rb-rule__title">Simulating NSFW actions using Animations</span><span class="rb-chips"><span class="rb-chip rb-chip--strike"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-timer" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M10 2h4M12 14l3-3"/><circle cx="12" cy="14" r="8"/></svg><span>Strike ban</span></span><span class="rb-chip rb-chip--ex"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-chevron-down" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"/></svg></span></summary><div class="rb-rule__body"><div class="rb-rule__inner"><div class="rb-explain"><p class="rb-explain__head"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" another player.</p></div><div class="rb-compare"><div class="rb-side rb-side--ban"><p class="rb-side__head"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-circle-x" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><path d="m15 9-6 6M9 9l6 6"/></svg><span>Bannable</span><span class="rb-side__n">4</span></p><ul class="rb-exs"><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Simulating NSFW actions by animating your body</span><span class="rb-sub"><span class="rb-sub__head">For example</span><ul><li><span class="rb-sub__t">Dancing around a pole, pretending to be a stripper on a stripper pole.</span></li></ul></span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">2 people using emotes to perform sexual acts together.</span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Using an emote to perform a sexual act on someone, with clear intent that it&#x27;s being used in a sexual way.</span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">W+S&#x27;ing another player who is using an emote in a sexual way, even if you aren&#x27;t doing any emote yourself.</span></li></ul></div><div class="rb-side rb-side--ok"><p class="rb-side__head"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-circle-check" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><path d="m16 9-5.5 5.5L8 12"/></svg><span>Not bannable</span><span class="rb-side__n">3</span></p><ul class="rb-exs"><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">W+S&#x27;ing another player, unless you use an animation or play audios that hint you&#x27;re doing an explicit action</span><span class="rb-ex__d">W+S&#x27;ing on its own, without those, is too tame to warrant a ban.</span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Using an emote that can be used as a sexual emote, but not near any player</span><span class="rb-ex__d">Emotes on their own can be normal dances. They only become bannable when used in a sexual way with another player.</span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Being AFK in an emote that can be used sexually, when a player walks up and performs sexual acts on you</span><span class="rb-ex__d">You weren&#x27;t actively taking part, so only the player who walked up to you is banned.</span></li></ul></div></div><div class="rb-counts"><p class="rb-counts__head">Counts everywhere</p><span class="rb-count"><b><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-person-standing" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="5" r="1"/><path d="m9 20 3-6 3 6M6 8l6 2 6-2M12 10v4"/></svg>Emote / Body Movement</b> means Emotes, Items that Move Your Arm, VR Movement, anything that allows you to move your body, etc</span></div><div class="rb-rule__foot"><span class="rb-punish"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-gavel" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m14 13-8.381 8.38a1 1 0 0 1-3.001-3l8.384-8.381M16 16l6-6M21.5 10.5l-8-8M8 8l6-6M8.5 7.5l8 8"/></svg><span>Strike ban: <b>3 days → 7 days → 30 days → permanent</b>. Breaking this same rule twice is permanent.</span></span><button type="button" class="rb-copy" data-code="a3"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-link" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.54.54l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7.07-7.07l-1.72 1.71"/><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 7.07l1.71-1.71"/></svg><span>Copy link</span></button></div></div></div></details><details class="rb-rule rb-rule--a" id="a4" data-search="a4 wearing inappropriate avatars  if an avatar breaks another rule, the ban is for that rule instead. for example: an outfit showing an evil person or organization breaks **b8**, so the ban is for **b8**. a shirt with graphic explicit content is nsfw imagery, so the ban is for **d1**. wearing no visible shirt and no visible pants with a realistic skin color   wearing clothes that imply you&#x27;d break a rule   wearing a sexual organ as part of your avatar.   wearing avatars of people or things originally from nsfw media or explicit content avatars of characters that were originally created for explicit content are bannable.  wearing avatars that show sexual body parts or explicit sexual references for example, a shirt with an explicit sexual phrase on it.  wearing avatars heavily associated with roblox condos   wearing very small items that only cover private areas, instead of clothing that goes around the body in the pictures, the first avatar (✗) is bannable and the second one (✓) is not.  wearing no visible shirt, but wearing pants, with a realistic skin color it&#x27;s only bannable when both are missing.  wearing no pants, but wearing a shirt, with a realistic skin color it&#x27;s only bannable when both are missing.  wearing no visible shirt and no visible pants with a skin color that isn&#x27;t realistic without a realistic skin color you aren&#x27;t trying to look like a real person, which is what this rule is about.  wearing a bypassed avatar that doesn&#x27;t break any rules a shirt with swear words is &quot;bypassed&quot; because roblox doesn&#x27;t normally allow it, but swearing isn&#x27;t bannable here, so the shirt isn&#x27;t either. it wouldn&#x27;t be fair to ban for something that is never banned otherwise.  wearing an avatar that shows drug use using or mentioning drugs is never a reason for a ban.  wearing a bikini without suggestive intent a bikini on its own is not bannable. "><summary><span class="rb-code">a4</span><span class="rb-rule__main"><span class="rb-rule__title">Wearing Inappropriate Avatars</span><span class="rb-chips"><span class="rb-chip rb-chip--strike"><svg 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 7.07l1.71-1.71"/></svg><span>Copy link</span></button></div></div></div></details><details class="rb-rule rb-rule--a" id="a5" data-search="a5 discussing and/or directing highly explicit content to a player   showing links to explicit sites through text or images  you post &quot;explicitsite.com&quot; in chat, or you have an image sign that says &quot;go to my explicitsite.com&quot;. showing icons or color schemes that reference explicit sites  you have an image with the icon of an explicit site. you use colors that are obviously meant to reference an explicit site. talking about highly explicit actions if it&#x27;s directed at someone (you say you&#x27;re doing it *to them*), it&#x27;s a ban for **d4** instead. telling someone you&#x27;re going to do an explicit action. talking about sexual positions. talking about sexual body parts  you say &quot;penis&quot;, &quot;vagina&quot; or &quot;boobs&quot;. hinting at a sexual phrase that would break a rule  you say &quot;pay gorn&quot;. it obviously means &quot;gay p*rn&quot;, which would be bannable. you wear a shirt that says &quot;i come in peace&quot; and someone else wears one that says &quot;i&#x27;m peace&quot;. together they obviously mean &quot;i c*m in them&quot;, so both players are banned. talking about sex toys.   talking about nude pictures.   calling someone, or yourself, a &quot;stress reliever&quot;.   talking about relationships without going into highly explicit details you can talk about your own relationships, as long as you don&#x27;t go into more detail than the conversation needs. you&#x27;re talking about your life and mention you were cheated on because your partner slept with other people. it refers to an explicit action, but in this context and without details, it&#x27;s fine. you mention you met someone and briefly say they were &quot;10/10 in bed&quot;. it refers to an explicit action, but you don&#x27;t go into details, so it&#x27;s fine. using a word that is a common insult but also means a sexual body part, as an insult or expression it only becomes bannable when you are talking about the body part itself. &quot;you&#x27;re such a d*ck&quot;. not bannable: it&#x27;s a common insult, and you aren&#x27;t talking about the body part. &quot;your d*ck is small&quot; or &quot;my d*ck is big&quot;. bannable: it&#x27;s an insult, but you are talking about the body part. &quot;i love d*ck&quot;. bannable: it isn&#x27;t an insult, and you are talking about the body part. &quot;d*ck&quot; on its own, with no other meaning. bannable: it isn&#x27;t an insult or an expression, so you are talking about the body part."><summary><span class="rb-code">a5</span><span class="rb-rule__main"><span class="rb-rule__title">Discussing and/or directing highly explicit content to a player</span><span class="rb-chips"><span class="rb-chip rb-chip--strike"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" Breaking this same rule twice is permanent.</span></span><button type="button" class="rb-copy" data-code="a5"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-link" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.54.54l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7.07-7.07l-1.72 1.71"/><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 7.07l1.71-1.71"/></svg><span>Copy link</span></button></div></div></div></details><details class="rb-rule rb-rule--a" id="a6" data-search="a6 participating in an activity with someone else who&#x27;s breaking rules   harassing another player together with someone who&#x27;s breaking rules  you and your friend walk up to a player and call them ugly. that alone is &quot;being mean&quot;, which isn&#x27;t bannable. then your friend starts calling them slurs while you keep calling them ugly: your friend is banned for **b3**, and because you are both harassing the same player, you are banned too, for **a6**. voluntarily taking a picture with players who are breaking rules  three players take a picture together. two of them are breaking a rule (**b8**), the third one isn&#x27;t but voluntarily stands with them for the picture. the two are banned for **b8**, and the third one for **a6**. voluntarily building with someone you know is breaking rules with their own build.   purposely drawing together with a player who&#x27;s breaking rules with their drawings (collaborative drawings).   harassing another player with your friend, when neither of you breaks any rules &quot;harassing&quot; another player doesn&#x27;t break any rules on its own, as long as nobody involved is breaking a rule.  harassing another player with your friend, then stopping completely when your friend starts breaking a rule your friend is banned, but you stopped as soon as they broke a rule, so you are no longer linked. "><summary><span class="rb-code">a6</span><span class="rb-rule__main"><span class="rb-rule__title">Participating in an activity with someone else who&#x27;s breaking rules</span><span class="rb-chips"><span class="rb-chip rb-chip--strike"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-timer" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M10 2h4M12 14l3-3"/><circle cx="12" cy="14" r="8"/></svg><span>Strike ban</span></span><span class="rb-chip rb-chip--ex"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-list-checks" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M13 lucide-chevron-down" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"/></svg></span></summary><div class="rb-rule__body"><div class="rb-rule__inner"><div class="rb-compare"><div class="rb-side rb-side--ban"><p class="rb-side__head"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-circle-x" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><path d="m15 9-6 6M9 9l6 6"/></svg><span>Bannable</span><span class="rb-side__n">4</span></p><ul class="rb-exs"><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Harassing another player together with someone who&#x27;s breaking rules</span><span class="rb-sub"><span class="rb-sub__head">For example</span><ul><li><span class="rb-sub__t">You and your friend walk up to a player and call them ugly.</span> <span class="rb-sub__why">That alone is &quot;being mean&quot;, which isn&#x27;t bannable. Then your friend starts calling them slurs while you keep calling them ugly: your friend is banned for <b>b3</b>, and because you are both harassing the same player, you are banned too, for <b>a6</b>.</span></li></ul></span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Voluntarily taking a picture with players who are breaking rules</span><span class="rb-sub"><span class="rb-sub__head">For example</span><ul><li><span class="rb-sub__t">Three players take a picture together.</span> <span class="rb-sub__why">Two of them are breaking a rule (<b>b8</b>), the third one isn&#x27;t but voluntarily stands with them for the picture. The two are banned for <b>b8</b>, and the third one for <b>a6</b>.</span></li></ul></span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Voluntarily building with someone you know is breaking rules with their own build.</span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Purposely drawing together with a player who&#x27;s breaking rules with their drawings (collaborative drawings).</span></li></ul></div><div class="rb-side rb-side--ok"><p class="rb-side__head"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-circle-check" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><path d="m16 9-5.5 5.5L8 12"/></svg><span>Not bannable</span><span class="rb-side__n">2</span></p><ul class="rb-exs"><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Harassing another player with your friend, when neither of you breaks any rules</span><span class="rb-ex__d">&quot;Harassing&quot; another player doesn&#x27;t break any rules on its own, as long as nobody involved is breaking a rule.</span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Harassing another player with your friend, then stopping completely when your friend starts breaking a rule</span><span class="rb-ex__d">Your friend is banned, but you stopped as soon as they broke a rule, so you are no longer linked.</span></li></ul></div></div><div class="rb-related"><p class="rb-related__q"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-circle-help" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><path d="M9.09 9a3 3 0 0 1 5.83 1c0 2-3 3-3 3M12 17h.01"/></svg><span>How do Build Related Rules work?</span></p><p>Players who collaborate on a build that contains bannable content will be banned, but only if they were aware of it. If a player joins a public build and secretly creates bannable content without others noticing, the other builders in that space are not held responsible. Banning someone requires that they knowingly allowed it to happen. However, if a player witnesses another builder creating bannable content and continues to build alongside them without reporting or stopping it, they are considered a willing participant and will be banned for the same reason as the offending player.</p></div><div class="rb-rule__foot"><span class="rb-punish"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-gavel" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m14 13-8.381 8.38a1 1 0 0 1-3.001-3l8.384-8.381M16 16l6-6M21.5 10.5l-8-8M8 8l6-6M8.5 7.5l8 8"/></svg><span>Strike ban: <b>3 days → 7 days → 30 days → permanent</b>. Breaking this same rule twice is permanent.</span></span><button type="button" class="rb-copy" data-code="a6"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-link" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.54.54l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7.07-7.07l-1.72 1.71"/><path d="M14 11a5 can get other players banned   telling someone to say something that breaks a rule  you tell someone to say &quot;book&quot; in lithuanian. that&#x27;s &quot;knyga&quot;, which obviously refers to the n-word, so in that context it&#x27;s bannable under **b3**. someone asks you for help talking to someone in spanish. you give them a sentence and say it means &quot;hello, how are you!&quot;, but it really means &quot;i want to f*ck you&quot;, which is bannable under **a5**. because you told them to say something that would get them banned, you are banned for **a7**. giving players the id of an image that would get them banned.   giving players the id of a sound that would get them banned.   having bannable content in your lasers  you have a bannable avatar inside your avatar laser. you have a bannable avatar item inside your avatar item laser."><summary><span class="rb-code">a7</span><span class="rb-rule__main"><span class="rb-rule__title">Doing something that can get other players someone is predatory with no valid reason   joking about another player liking minors  you say &quot;this person is here to meet a 13 year old girl!&quot; about another player for no valid reason. unless there is valid proof, it&#x27;s bannable. hinting that an entire group of people are pedophiles  you say &quot;all mods are pedophiles and like kids&quot;. you say &quot;all people from religion x are pedophiles and like kids&quot;. you say &quot;anyone who plays minecraft likes kids&quot;. joking about another player being in the epstein files.   hinting that someone protects pedophiles.   hinting that someone buys explicit pictures for robux, with no valid proof.   saying that someone who is provably a pedophile is a pedophile. there is a valid reason, so it&#x27;s not bannable.  saying that some people from a group are pedophiles, when it&#x27;s provably true it&#x27;s not bannable because it&#x27;s very likely true. you say &quot;roblox has a lot of pedophiles&quot;. given how many predators have been caught on roblox, it&#x27;s a valid statement. you say &quot;religion x has pedophiles&quot;. any religion has millions of people, so at least one is most likely a predator: it&#x27;s a valid statement."><summary><span class="rb-code">a8</span><span class="rb-rule__main"><span class="rb-rule__title">Insinuating that someone is predatory with no and using your age as the main reason someone should talk to you  you have a booth that only says &quot;i&#x27;m 13 yrs old&quot;. you are using the age of a minor (below 18) as the only reason someone should talk to you, so it&#x27;s bannable. actively looking for older people to have relationships with while you are a minor.   being a minor and lying about your age to seem older.   offering to sell your body, or to do explicit actions or fan signs, for anything.   showing full credit card info  you show the card number, the expiry date and the security code, like &quot;1234 5678 9012 3456 01/26 123&quot;. showing a full address that includes numbers  you show an address like &quot;1234 meetpeople, state, country&quot;. without the numbers, like &quot;meetpeople, state, country&quot;, it would be allowed. giving your social media to another person. giving out your social media is not bannable on its own.  having your social media account in your username or bio. giving out your social media is not bannable on its own.  having your age in your bio a bio just shares general information about you. something like a booth sign actively uses your age as a reason for people to come to you: the difference is the intent and the context.  offering to sell your artistic skills for anything. selling your art skills isn&#x27;t dangerous. this includes singing for players: it&#x27;s a skill you perform, not selling your body or anything explicit. <section class="rb-ch rb-ch--group rb-ch--b" id="rb-b" data-title="Hate and harm"><header class="rb-ch__head"><span class="rb-ch__badge rb-letter">b</span><div><span class="rb-ch__kicker">Chapter 3 · ban codes</span><h2 class="rb-ch__title">Hate and harm</h2><p class="rb-ch__about">Slurs, hate, discrimination and wishing harm on people.</p></div></header><div class="rb-banner rb-banner--strike"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-timer" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M10 2h4M12 14l3-3"/><circle cx="12" cy="14" r="8"/></svg><span><b>Strike bans:</b> 3 days → 7 days → 30 days → permanent. <a href="#rb-start">How bans work</a></span></div><div class="rb-rules"><details class="rb-rule rb-rule--b" id="b3" data-search="b3 referring to or saying a racial / homophobic slur, or the insults r*tard, sl*t or wh*re   saying a racial or homophobic slur.   saying a word that clearly tries to reference a racial or homophobic slur  you say &quot;neck hurt&quot; while clearly referring to the hard-r n-word. you set your name to &quot;nick gurr&quot;, which clearly references the hard-r n-word. saying &quot;n*gga&quot; when it isn&#x27;t part of a conversation  you wear clothes that refer to or say &quot;n*gga&quot;. it isn&#x27;t used in a conversation, so it counts as saying a racist slur. using &quot;n*gga&quot; as a slur  you tell someone &quot;you&#x27;re a dirty n*gga&quot;, and from the context it isn&#x27;t because they haven&#x27;t showered but because they&#x27;re black. even in a conversation, this is like using it as a slur, so it&#x27;s bannable. saying r*tard, sl*t or wh*re.   saying &quot;n*gga&quot; in a conversation in a conversation it doesn&#x27;t carry the same weight as directly calling someone a slur. you&#x27;re talking to your friend and say &quot;yo n*gga what&#x27;s good&quot;. it&#x27;s clearly part of the conversation and not meant as a slur. saying &quot;b*tch&quot;. it&#x27;s not a racial or homophobic slur, so it&#x27;s not bannable.  saying &quot;f*ck&quot;. it&#x27;s not a racial or homophobic slur, so it&#x27;s not bannable.  saying &quot;moid&quot; or &quot;foid&quot;. they&#x27;re not racial or homophobic slurs, so they&#x27;re not bannable. "><summary><span class="rb-code">b3</span><span class="rb-rule__main"><span class="rb-rule__title">Referring to or saying a racial / homophobic slur, or the insults r*tard, sl*t or wh*re</span><span class="rb-chips"><span class="rb-chip rb-chip--strike"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-timer" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M10 2h4M12 14l3-3"/><circle cx="12" cy="14" r="8"/></svg><span>Strike ban</span></span><span class="rb-chip rb-chip--ex"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-list-checks" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M13 5h8M13 12h8M13 19h8M3 17l2 2 4-4M3 7l2 2 4-4"/></svg><span>9 examples</span></span></span></span><span class="rb-rule__toggle" aria-hidden="true"><span class="rb-rule__open">Open</span><span class="rb-rule__close">Close</span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-chevron-down" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"/></svg></span></summary><div class="rb-rule__body"><div class="rb-rule__inner"><div class="rb-compare"><div class="rb-side rb-side--ban"><p class="rb-side__head"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-circle-x" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><path d="m15 9-6 6M9 9l6 6"/></svg><span>Bannable</span><span class="rb-side__n">5</span></p><ul class="rb-exs"><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Saying a racial or homophobic slur.</span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Saying a word that clearly tries to reference a racial or homophobic slur</span><span class="rb-sub"><span class="rb-sub__head">2 examples</span><ul><li><span class="rb-sub__t">You say <span class="rb-say">Neck Hurt</span> while clearly referring to the hard-R N-word.</span></li><li><span class="rb-sub__t">You set your name to &quot;Nick Gurr&quot;, which clearly references the hard-R N-word.</span></li></ul></span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Saying <span class="rb-say">n*gga</span> when it isn&#x27;t part of a conversation</span><span class="rb-sub"><span class="rb-sub__head">For example</span><ul><li><span class="rb-sub__t">You wear clothes that refer to or say <span class="rb-say">n*gga</span>.</span> <span class="rb-sub__why">It isn&#x27;t used in a conversation, so it counts as saying a racist slur.</span></li></ul></span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Using &quot;n*gga&quot; as a slur</span><span class="rb-sub"><span class="rb-sub__head">For example</span><ul><li><span class="rb-sub__t">You tell someone <span class="rb-say">You&#x27;re a dirty n*gga</span>, and from the context it isn&#x27;t because they haven&#x27;t showered but because they&#x27;re black.</span> <span class="rb-sub__why">Even in a conversation, this is like using it as a slur, so it&#x27;s bannable.</span></li></ul></span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Saying r*tard, sl*t or wh*re.</span></li></ul></div><div class="rb-side rb-side--ok"><p class="rb-side__head"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-circle-check" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><path d="m16 9-5.5 5.5L8 12"/></svg><span>Not bannable</span><span class="rb-side__n">4</span></p><ul class="rb-exs"><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Saying <span class="rb-say">n*gga</span> in a conversation</span><span class="rb-ex__d">In a conversation it doesn&#x27;t carry the same weight as directly calling someone a slur.</span><span class="rb-sub"><span class="rb-sub__head">For example</span><ul><li><span class="rb-sub__t">You&#x27;re talking to your friend and say <span class="rb-say">Yo n*gga what&#x27;s good</span>.</span> <span class="rb-sub__why">It&#x27;s clearly part of the conversation and not meant as a slur.</span></li></ul></span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Saying <span class="rb-say">b*tch</span>.</span><span class="rb-ex__d">It&#x27;s not a racial or homophobic slur, so it&#x27;s not bannable.</span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Saying <span class="rb-say">f*ck</span>.</span><span class="rb-ex__d">It&#x27;s not a racial or homophobic slur, so it&#x27;s not bannable.</span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Saying <span class="rb-say">moid</span> or <span class="rb-say">foid</span>.</span><span class="rb-ex__d">They&#x27;re not racial or homophobic slurs, so they&#x27;re not bannable.</span></li></ul></div></div><div class="rb-counts"><p class="rb-counts__head">Counts everywhere</p><span class="rb-count"><b><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-message-circle" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M2.992 16.342a2 2 0 0 1 .094 1.167l-1.065 3.29a1 1 0 0 0 1.236 1.168l3.413-.998a2 2 0 0 1 1.099.092 10 10 0 1 0-4.777-4.719"/></svg>Say</b> means Text Chat, Voice Chat, Signs, Images, Written Text in the Draw Area, Bios, Usernames, Words found on anything you wear, Comments to other Players, etc</span></div><div class="rb-rule__foot"><span class="rb-punish"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-gavel" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m14 13-8.381 8.38a1 1 0 0 1-3.001-3l8.384-8.381M16 16l6-6M21.5 10.5l-8-8M8 8l6-6M8.5 7.5l8 8"/></svg><span>Strike ban: <b>3 days → 7 days → 30 days → permanent</b>. Breaking this same rule twice is permanent.</span></span><button type="button" class="rb-copy" data-code="b3"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-link" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.54.54l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7.07-7.07l-1.72 1.71"/><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 7.07l1.71-1.71"/></svg><span>Copy link</span></button></div></div></div></details><details class="rb-rule rb-rule--b" id="b4" data-search="b4 telling / implying someone to harm themselves   telling someone to kill themselves (kys).   hinting that you mean to tell someone to kill themselves  you and another player are insulting each other, and you say &quot;please keep yourself safe! kys, as in keeping yourself safe!&quot;. it obviously means you want them to kill themselves, so it&#x27;s bannable. telling someone to cut themselves or slit their wrists.   telling someone to go die.   telling someone they need to die.   telling someone to go to hell. it doesn&#x27;t imply they should harm themselves.  saying any of the bannable examples to a verified criminal who has been jailed they are people too, but because they commit crimes, wishing them harm is allowed. someone is provably a pedophile and you tell them to die. this isn&#x27;t bannable."><summary><span class="rb-code">b4</span><span class="rb-rule__main"><span class="rb-rule__title">Telling / Implying someone to harm themselves</span><span class="rb-chips"><span class="rb-chip rb-chip--strike"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-timer" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M10 2h4M12 14l3-3"/><circle cx="12" cy="14" r="8"/></svg><span>Strike ban</span></span><span class="rb-chip rb-chip--ex"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-list-checks" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M13 5h8M13 12h8M13 19h8M3 17l2 2 4-4M3 7l2 2 4-4"/></svg><span>7 examples</span></span></span></span><span class="rb-rule__toggle" aria-hidden="true"><span class="rb-rule__open">Open</span><span class="rb-rule__close">Close</span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-chevron-down" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"/></svg></span></summary><div class="rb-rule__body"><div class="rb-rule__inner"><div class="rb-compare"><div class="rb-side rb-side--ban"><p class="rb-side__head"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-circle-x" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><path d="m15 9-6 6M9 9l6 6"/></svg><span>Bannable</span><span class="rb-side__n">5</span></p><ul class="rb-exs"><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Telling someone to kill example</span><ul><li><span class="rb-sub__t">You and another player are insulting each other, and you say <span class="rb-say">Please keep yourself safe! KYS, as in keeping yourself safe!</span>.</span> <span class="rb-sub__why">It obviously means you want them to kill themselves, so it&#x27;s bannable.</span></li></ul></span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Telling someone to cut themselves or slit their wrists.</span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Telling someone to go die.</span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Telling someone they need to die.</span></li></ul></div><div class="rb-side rb-side--ok"><p class="rb-side__head"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-circle-check" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><path d="m16 9-5.5 5.5L8 12"/></svg><span>Not bannable</span><span class="rb-side__n">2</span></p><ul class="rb-exs"><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Telling someone to go to hell.</span><span class="rb-ex__d">It doesn&#x27;t imply they should harm themselves.</span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Saying any of the bannable examples to a verified criminal who has been jailed</span><span class="rb-ex__d">They are people too, but because they commit crimes, wishing them harm is allowed.</span><span class="rb-sub"><span class="rb-sub__head">For example</span><ul><li><span class="rb-sub__t">Someone is provably a pedophile and you tell them to die.</span> <span class="rb-sub__why">This isn&#x27;t bannable.</span></li></ul></span></li></ul></div></div><div class="rb-rule__foot"><span class="rb-punish"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-gavel" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m14 13-8.381 8.38a1 1 0 0 1-3.001-3l8.384-8.381M16 16l6-6M21.5 10.5l-8-8M8 8l6-6M8.5 7.5l8 8"/></svg><span>Strike ban: <b>3 days → 7 days → 30 days → permanent</b>. stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-link" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.54.54l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7.07-7.07l-1.72 1.71"/><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 7.07l1.71-1.71"/></svg><span>Copy link</span></button></div></div></div></details><details class="rb-rule rb-rule--b" id="b5" data-search="b5 wishing / joking about death / elimination of a race / country / ethnicity / person / religion  if a player is talking about another player in the game, the ban is for **b4** instead. this rule is for groups of people, or people outside the game. wishing, joking or drawing about the death or elimination of a race.   wishing, joking or drawing about the death or elimination of a country  you say &quot;they need to nuke country x off the map&quot;. you say &quot;everyone in country x needs to die&quot;. wishing, joking or drawing about the death or elimination of an ethnicity.   wishing, joking or drawing about the death or elimination of a religion.   wishing, joking or drawing about the death or elimination of specific people.   wishing, joking or drawing about the death of a real person who hasn&#x27;t committed a crime and been jailed for it  you say &quot;they need to kill this politician because i don&#x27;t like them&quot;, but the politician hasn&#x27;t committed any crimes, so it&#x27;s bannable. you say &quot;i&#x27;m glad this person is dead!&quot;, but the person hadn&#x27;t committed any crimes, so it&#x27;s bannable. you say you hope a real politician dies. whether or not you believe they&#x27;re a criminal, they haven&#x27;t been jailed, so it&#x27;s bannable. wishing, joking or drawing about a country being entirely wiped out and replaced with a different country  you say &quot;country x needs to be wiped off the map and fully replaced with country y&quot;. images showing any flag or religious symbol being burned.   realistic images of real people tarnishing any flag or religious symbol only realistic images count, like photos of real people damaging or disrespecting a flag or a religious symbol.  wishes, jokes or drawings about the death or elimination of something, when they aren&#x27;t targeted they aren&#x27;t targeted at anyone, so they affect no one specifically. you say &quot;they need to nuke earth&quot;. it isn&#x27;t targeted at anyone, so it&#x27;s not bannable. you say &quot;i hate these chairs, they hurt my back, i hope every chair like this gets blown up&quot;. it&#x27;s about an object nobody has a cultural attachment to (like they would to a country), so it&#x27;s not bannable. wishing, joking or drawing about a country being merged with another country it&#x27;s not on the same level as asking for a country to be completely eliminated. you say &quot;country x should merge and become a state of country y&quot;. wishing, joking or drawing about the death of a fictional person. fictional people aren&#x27;t real, so it doesn&#x27;t carry the same weight.  wishing, joking or drawing about the death of verified criminals, or people who committed crimes and were jailed they are people too, but because they commit crimes, wishing for their death is allowed. you say &quot;all rapists should die&quot;. they are criminals, so it&#x27;s not bannable. you say &quot;all pedophiles should die&quot;. they are criminals, so it&#x27;s not bannable. you say &quot;person x is a murderer and i wish they would die already&quot;. if person x is verifiably a murderer and was jailed for it, it&#x27;s not bannable. wishing, joking or drawing about your own death. joking about yourself is fine.  saying &quot;country x owns country y&quot;. on its own this is too tame for a ban.  images of a flag with an ❌ on it, without comparing it to another flag compared with another flag it would be bannable, for **b6**: discrimination.  jumping or walking on a flag or religious symbol in the game  "><summary><span class="rb-code">b5</span><span class="rb-rule__main"><span class="rb-rule__title">Wishing / Joking about death / elimination of a race / country / ethnicity / person / religion</span><span class="rb-chips"><span class="rb-chip rb-chip--strike"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" fine.</span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Saying <span class="rb-say">Country X owns Country Y</span>.</span><span class="rb-ex__d">On its own this is too tame for a ban.</span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Images of a flag with an ❌ on it, without comparing it to another flag</span><span class="rb-ex__d">Compared with another flag it would be bannable, for <b>b6</b>: Discrimination.</span><span class="rb-pics"><button type="button" class="rb-pic rb-pic--ok" data-src="../assets/rules/b6-ok-country-cross.webp" data-cap="Not bannable: one flag with a red cross, not compared to another country" data-mark="ok" data-rule="b5"><img src="../assets/rules/b6-ok-country-cross.webp" alt="Not bannable: one flag with a red cross, not compared to another country" loading="lazy"><span class="rb-pic__mark" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="rb-pic rb-pic--ok" data-src="../assets/rules/b5-ok-walk-flag.webp" data-cap="A player jumping on a drawing of a flag in the Draw Area" data-mark="ok" data-rule="b5"><img 17l-5-5"/></svg></span></button></span></li></ul></div></div><div class="rb-counts"><p class="rb-counts__head">Counts everywhere</p><span class="rb-count"><b><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-earth" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M21.54 15H17a2 2 0 0 0-2 2v4.54M7 3.34V5a3 3 8"/></svg><span>Strike ban: <b>3 days → 7 days → 30 days → permanent</b>. Breaking this same rule twice is permanent.</span></span><button type="button" class="rb-copy" data-code="b5"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-link" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.54.54l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7.07-7.07l-1.72 1.71"/><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 7.07l1.71-1.71"/></svg><span>Copy link</span></button></div></div></div></details><details class="rb-rule rb-rule--b" id="b6" data-search="b6 discrimination   saying negative stereotypes about an entire race, ethnicity or sexuality (except about weight)  calling someone from iraq a suicide bomber. it&#x27;s a negative stereotype: being a suicide bomber isn&#x27;t a good thing, and you&#x27;re assuming they must be one because they&#x27;re from iraq. going up to a black person and saying &quot;do you want kfc and watermelon?&quot;. black people liking kfc or watermelon has racist roots, so it&#x27;s a negative stereotype and bannable. going up to an asian person and saying &quot;ch*ng ch*ng&quot;. asian people don&#x27;t talk like that and it has racist roots, so it&#x27;s bannable. seeing an indian player and calling them stinky straight away. treating every indian person as stinky, as if they&#x27;re born that way, is a negative stereotype, so it&#x27;s bannable. mimicking an indian accent while talking about running a scam call center. you&#x27;re assuming all indians run call centers, which is a negative stereotype, so it&#x27;s bannable. going up to spanish people and calling them illegals. you&#x27;re assuming every spanish person is an illegal immigrant, so it&#x27;s bannable. saying you don&#x27;t like a race, ethnicity, sexuality, or the people of a religion  you say &quot;i don&#x27;t like black people&quot;. you say &quot;i don&#x27;t like gay people&quot;. you say &quot;i don&#x27;t like white people&quot;. you say &quot;i don&#x27;t like christians&quot;. saying &quot;i don&#x27;t like christianity&quot; is fine. hinting that a race, ethnicity, sexuality or religion isn&#x27;t normal.   hinting that someone is less than human  you say &quot;black people are less than human&quot;. you call black people &quot;chocolate people&quot;. you call mexicans &quot;taco people&quot;. you call japanese people &quot;anime people&quot;. you go up to a group of japanese players and say &quot;hello ramen people!&quot;. you walk up to a black player and say &quot;hello black&quot;. you say &quot;f*ck you gay&quot;. you say &quot;f*ck you black&quot;. you go up to a woman and say &quot;go to the kitchen and make me a sandwich&quot;. you ask someone &quot;are you colored or normal?&quot;. it implies that only white people are normal, so it&#x27;s bannable. you make images that rank skin colors, like a &quot;tier list&quot; of skin tones, or a chart of skin colors marked okay and not okay. using an image of a race, sexuality or religion with an ❌ on it   using an image of a country with an ❌ on it, while comparing it to another country   wearing an avatar meant to be a racist caricature  a racist caricature of a black person: very black skin and bright red lips. a racist caricature of an asian person: yellow skin, slanted eyes and a straw hat. calling first nations people indian.   calling trans people transformers.   mocking a religion  you call the mecca a minecraft block. you call allah &quot;pig allah&quot;. you mock a religious saying, for example by saying &quot;outshallah&quot; (the opposite of inshallah). you wear a religious avatar in a way that mocks the religion. declaring a religion fake, false or invalid, outside of a structured debate.   denying that a real country recognized in the game exists  you say &quot;israel isn&#x27;t a real country&quot;. you say &quot;palestine isn&#x27;t a real country&quot;. taunting a player about politics only because of their nationality, ethnicity, religion or gender, outside of a structured debate  you go up to a random player from palestine and say &quot;free israel!&quot;. the phrase isn&#x27;t bannable on its own, but saying it to a random person from palestine is. you go up to a random player from israel and say &quot;free palestine!&quot;. the phrase isn&#x27;t bannable on its own, but saying it to a random person from israel is. you go up to a random black player and say &quot;do you love george floyd?&quot;. saying &quot;ch*ng ch*ng&quot;.   using an image of a country with an ❌ on it, without comparing it to another country on its own it&#x27;s like saying &quot;i don&#x27;t like country x&quot;, which is not bannable.  talking about the food of a culture saying how certain cultures eat certain foods isn&#x27;t discrimination. you go up to someone from france and say &quot;how often do you eat baguettes?&quot;. you walk up to someone from quebec and say &quot;is poutine really that good?&quot;. you go to a canadian and say &quot;do you really eat maple syrup with everything?&quot;. you go to a japanese player and ask &quot;is ramen actually that good?&quot;. saying you don&#x27;t like a country or a religion.   saying &quot;f*ck country x!&quot; it&#x27;s like saying you don&#x27;t like a country, which is allowed.  saying you don&#x27;t like people from a country.   calling a country poor insulting a country by calling it poor is too tame for a ban.  saying you wouldn&#x27;t date people of a race, ethnicity, sexuality or religion a dating preference doesn&#x27;t mean you see them as less than human or not normal. you say &quot;me personally, i wouldn&#x27;t date an x person&quot;. you say &quot;i&#x27;m not into x people&quot;. calling someone fat as an insult it&#x27;s too tame for a ban.  generalizing about the weight of a whole race, ethnicity or sexuality insults about weight are too tame for a ban. you say &quot;everyone in country x is fat&quot;. saying positive stereotypes about a race, ethnicity or sexuality this isn&#x27;t discrimination: it&#x27;s positive. you say &quot;you&#x27;re black so you must be really athletic!&quot;. you say &quot;you&#x27;re asian so you must be really smart!&quot;. you say &quot;you&#x27;re from dubai, you must be really rich!&quot;. mimicking an accent fine on its own, as long as you don&#x27;t say bannable things with it.  playing stereotypical songs of any culture fine on its own, as long as nothing bannable is said with it.  wearing the avatar of a religious figure fine, as long as you aren&#x27;t mocking it.  saying you don&#x27;t believe in a religion fine, as long as you aren&#x27;t mocking it.  saying you don&#x27;t like things that a race, ethnicity, sexuality or religion does fine, as long as what you say doesn&#x27;t break another rule. you say &quot;i don&#x27;t like how christians are so strict&quot;. you say &quot;i personally don&#x27;t like asian food&quot;. you say &quot;i don&#x27;t like jewish songs&quot;. you say &quot;i don&#x27;t like mexican food&quot;. saying a country is fake as a joke like saying &quot;you&#x27;re not indonesian, you&#x27;re polish&quot;, a joke about the two flags being upside down versions of each other.  saying a country is sh*t it&#x27;s like saying you don&#x27;t like a country, which isn&#x27;t bannable. about the food of a culture</span><span class="rb-ex__d">Saying how certain cultures eat certain foods isn&#x27;t discrimination.</span><span class="rb-sub"><span class="rb-sub__head">4 examples</span><ul><li><span class="rb-sub__t">You go up to someone from France and say <span class="rb-say">How often do you eat baguettes?</span>.</span></li><li><span class="rb-sub__t">You walk up to someone from Quebec and say <span class="rb-say">Is poutine really that good?</span>.</span></li><li><span class="rb-sub__t">You go to a Canadian and say <span class="rb-say">Do you really eat maple syrup with everything?</span>.</span></li><li><span class="rb-sub__t">You go to a Japanese player and ask <span class="rb-say">Is ramen actually that good?</span>.</span></li></ul></span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Saying you don&#x27;t like a country or a religion.</span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Saying <span class="rb-say">F*ck Country X!</span></span><span class="rb-ex__d">It&#x27;s like saying you don&#x27;t like a country, which is allowed.</span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Saying you don&#x27;t like people from a country.</span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Calling a country poor</span><span class="rb-ex__d">Insulting a country by calling it poor is too tame for a ban.</span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Saying you wouldn&#x27;t date people of a race, ethnicity, sexuality or religion</span><span class="rb-ex__d">A dating preference doesn&#x27;t mean you see them as less than human or not normal.</span><span class="rb-sub"><span class="rb-sub__head">2 examples</span><ul><li><span class="rb-sub__t">You say <span class="rb-say">Me personally, I wouldn&#x27;t date an X person</span>.</span></li><li><span class="rb-sub__t">You say <span class="rb-say">I&#x27;m not into X people</span>.</span></li></ul></span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Calling someone fat as an insult</span><span class="rb-ex__d">It&#x27;s too tame for a ban.</span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Generalizing about the weight of a whole race, ethnicity or sexuality</span><span class="rb-ex__d">Insults about weight are too tame for a ban.</span><span class="rb-sub"><span class="rb-sub__head">For example</span><ul><li><span class="rb-sub__t">You say <span class="rb-say">Everyone in Country X is fat</span>.</span></li></ul></span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Saying positive stereotypes about a race, ethnicity or sexuality</span><span class="rb-ex__d">This isn&#x27;t discrimination: it&#x27;s positive.</span><span class="rb-sub"><span class="rb-sub__head">3 examples</span><ul><li><span class="rb-sub__t">You say <span class="rb-say">You&#x27;re Black so you must be really athletic!</span>.</span></li><li><span class="rb-sub__t">You say <span class="rb-say">You&#x27;re Asian so you must be really smart!</span>.</span></li><li><span class="rb-sub__t">You say <span class="rb-say">You&#x27;re from Dubai, you must be really rich!</span>.</span></li></ul></span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Mimicking an accent</span><span class="rb-ex__d">Fine on its own, as long as you don&#x27;t say bannable things with it.</span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Playing stereotypical songs of any culture</span><span class="rb-ex__d">Fine on its own, as long as nothing bannable is said with it.</span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Wearing the avatar of a religious figure</span><span class="rb-ex__d">Fine, as long as you aren&#x27;t mocking it.</span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Saying you don&#x27;t believe in a religion</span><span class="rb-ex__d">Fine, as long as you aren&#x27;t mocking it.</span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Saying you don&#x27;t like things that a race, ethnicity, sexuality or religion does</span><span class="rb-ex__d">Fine, as long as what you say doesn&#x27;t break another rule.</span><span class="rb-sub"><span class="rb-sub__head">4 examples</span><ul><li><span class="rb-sub__t">You say <span class="rb-say">I don&#x27;t like how Christians are so strict</span>.</span></li><li><span class="rb-sub__t">You say <span class="rb-say">I personally don&#x27;t like Asian food</span>.</span></li><li><span class="rb-sub__t">You say <span class="rb-say">I don&#x27;t like Jewish songs</span>.</span></li><li><span class="rb-sub__t">You say <span class="rb-say">I don&#x27;t like Mexican food</span>.</span></li></ul></span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Saying a country is fake as a joke</span><span class="rb-ex__d">Like saying <span class="rb-say">You&#x27;re not Indonesian, you&#x27;re Polish</span>, a joke about the two flags being upside down versions of each other.</span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Saying a country is sh*t</span><span class="rb-ex__d">It&#x27;s like saying you don&#x27;t like a country, which isn&#x27;t bannable.</span></li></ul></div></div><div class="rb-counts"><p class="rb-counts__head">Counts everywhere</p><span class="rb-count"><b><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" class="rb-rule__foot"><span class="rb-punish"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide Breaking this same rule twice is permanent.</span></span><button type="button" class="rb-copy" data-code="b6"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-link" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.54.54l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7.07-7.07l-1.72 1.71"/><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 7.07l1.71-1.71"/></svg><span>Copy link</span></button></div></div></div></details><details class="rb-rule rb-rule--b" id="b8" data-search="b8 depicting nazi / terrorists / kkk / axis / zov / dead dictators / ussr / pedos / hate figures  **evil people** means people who support the beliefs of, or are the embodiment of: nazis, terrorists, the kkk (white supremacist groups), the axis, zov, dead dictators, the ussr, cartels and hate figures. **hate figures** are people who actively support the beliefs of an evil person, or who are known purely for breaking a rule from any ban code. pedophiles are banned for **d4** instead. using words commonly tied to evil people with no conversation, or no context that gives them another meaning, counts as supporting them. for example, the username &quot;blitzkrieg&quot;, or &quot;blitzkrieg&quot; written on a text sign or in the draw area with no context, embodies the nazis and is bannable. wearing an avatar that represents an evil person  an avatar dressed like the kkk, a white supremacist group. an avatar dressed as hitler, the leader of the nazis. an avatar meant to look like a tiktoker who became popular purely for saying racial slurs. slurs break **b3**, and they are known for breaking it, so they count as a hate figure. having a username or display name that represents an evil person.   showing support for the ideology of an evil person.   showing symbols that represent an evil person.   showing pictures of an evil person.   singing songs that represent, or are heavily tied to, an evil person.   supporting the beliefs or actions of any dictator, alive or dead.   talking about evil people without saying you support them talking about them doesn&#x27;t mean you actively support their beliefs.  depicting dictators or politicians who are alive today, like kim jong un or maduro it wouldn&#x27;t be fair: north korea is a dictatorship, and it&#x27;s a country admins can pretend to be from.  wearing ice avatars ice avatars aren&#x27;t bannable on their own. going up to spanish people and saying &quot;you&#x27;re an illegal&quot; would be bannable for **b6**: discrimination.  depicting gangs like the crips or the bloods gangs aren&#x27;t on the same level as terrorists or cartels.  talking about or supporting communism the ussr is bannable, but not the idea of communism. "><summary><span class="rb-code">b8</span><span class="rb-rule__main"><span class="rb-rule__title">Depicting Nazi / Terrorists / KKK / Axis / Zov / Dead Dictators / USSR / Pedos / Hate beliefs of, or are the embodiment of: Nazis, terrorists, the KKK (white supremacist groups), the Axis, Zov, dead dictators, the USSR, cartels and hate figures.</p><p><b>Hate figures</b> are people who actively support the beliefs of an Evil Person, or who are known purely for breaking a rule from any ban code. Pedophiles are banned for <b>d4</b> instead.</p><p>Using words commonly tied to Evil People with no conversation, or no context that gives them another meaning, counts as supporting them. For example, the username &quot;Blitzkrieg&quot;, or &quot;Blitzkrieg&quot; written on a Text Sign or in the Draw Area with no context, embodies the Nazis and is bannable.</p></div><div class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Playing audios of gross or bodily noises (burping, screaming, farting, bowel-movement sounds, etc.)</span><span class="rb-ex__d">It&#x27;s not nearly on the same level as real-life crude images.</span></li></ul></div></div><div class="rb-counts"><p class="rb-counts__head">Counts everywhere</p><span class="rb-count"><b><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-image" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><rect width="18" height="18" x="3" y="3" rx="2" Builds you participated in, Stuff found on the Clothes you wear, Camera Booth Pictures, etc</span></div><div class="rb-rule__foot"><span class="rb-punish"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" <section class="rb-ch rb-ch--group rb-ch--c" id="rb-c" data-title="Cheating, spam and fake staff"><header class="rb-ch__head"><span class="rb-ch__badge rb-letter">c</span><div><span class="rb-ch__kicker">Chapter 4 · ban codes</span><h2 class="rb-ch__title">Cheating, spam and fake staff</h2><p class="rb-ch__about">Pretending to be staff, cheating, exploits, spam and false reports.</p></div></header><div class="rb-banner rb-banner--strike"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-timer" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M10 2h4M12 14l3-3"/><circle cx="12" cy="14" r="8"/></svg><span><b>Strike bans:</b> 3 days → 7 days → 30 days → permanent. <a href="#rb-start">How bans work</a></span></div><div class="rb-rules"><details class="rb-rule rb-rule--c" id="c1" data-search="c1 pretending to being a mod / having mod powers / mini-modding  **staff** means any of these words: **mod**, **admin**, **head admin**, **owner (nogo)**, **staff**, **mini-mod**. saying &quot;i can ban you when i want&quot;.   saying you are staff.   saying &quot;i&#x27;m friends with a [staff]&quot; to make a player believe you have power.   having a staff title as your display name or region.   saying you have admin powers.   saying you&#x27;ll constantly watch someone to make sure they follow the rules, when you aren&#x27;t staff like following them from server to server and making it clear you only join their servers to check on them.  telling a player you can get them unbanned if they do something for you  you report a player and they get banned. then you tell them you&#x27;ll get them unbanned if they &quot;send you robux&quot; or &quot;send ban proof on other users&quot;. if the &quot;something&quot; is explicit, the ban is for **d4** instead. having admin or mod as your username, when your display name covers it you aren&#x27;t showing it to others, so you aren&#x27;t actively making people think you&#x27;re staff. "><summary><span class="rb-code">c1</span><span class="rb-rule__main"><span class="rb-rule__title">Pretending to being a Mod / having Mod powers / Mini-Modding</span><span class="rb-chips"><span class="rb-chip rb-chip--strike"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-timer" game</span></span><span class="rb-chip rb-chip--ex"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-list-checks" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M13 5h8M13 12h8M13 19h8M3 17l2 2 4-4M3 7l2 2 4-4"/></svg><span>8 examples</span></span></span></span><span class="rb-rule__toggle" <b>Mod</b>, <b>Admin</b>, <b>Head Admin</b>, <b>Owner (Nogo)</b>, <b>Staff</b>, <b>Mini-Mod</b>.</p></div><div class="rb-compare"><div class="rb-side rb-side--ban"><p class="rb-side__head"><svg class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Saying you have Admin powers.</span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Saying you&#x27;ll constantly watch someone to make sure they follow the rules, when you aren&#x27;t Staff</span><span class="rb-ex__d">Like following them from server to server and making it clear you only join their servers to check on them.</span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Telling a player you can get them unbanned if they do something for you</span><span class="rb-sub"><span class="rb-sub__head">For example</span><ul><li><span class="rb-sub__t">You report a player and they get banned.</span> <span class="rb-sub__why">Then you tell them you&#x27;ll get them unbanned if they <span class="rb-say">Send you Robux</span> or <span class="rb-say">Send ban proof on other users</span>. If the &quot;something&quot; is explicit, the ban is for <b>d4</b> instead.</span></li></ul></span></li></ul></div><div class="rb-side rb-side--ok"><p class="rb-side__head"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-circle-check" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><path d="m16 9-5.5 5.5L8 12"/></svg><span>Not bannable</span><span class="rb-side__n">1</span></p><ul class="rb-exs"><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Having Admin or Mod as your username, when your display name covers it</span><span class="rb-ex__d">You aren&#x27;t showing it to others, so you aren&#x27;t actively making people think you&#x27;re Staff.</span></li></ul></div></div><div class="rb-rule__foot"><span class="rb-punish"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-gavel" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m14 13-8.381 8.38a1 1 0 0 1-3.001-3l8.384-8.381M16 16l6-6M21.5 10.5l-8-8M8 8l6-6M8.5 7.5l8 8"/></svg><span>Strike ban: <b>3 days → 7 days → 30 days → permanent</b>. Breaking this same rule twice is permanent.</span></span><button type="button" class="rb-copy" data-code="c1"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-link" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.54.54l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7.07-7.07l-1.72 1.71"/><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 7.07l1.71-1.71"/></svg><span>Copy link</span></button></div></div></div></details><details class="rb-rule rb-rule--c" id="c2" data-search="c2 using a method to fake donations that appears in data will get you data wiped found a bug like this? report it to nogo with a **major bug report** ticket on the official discord: you get a reward instead of a ban. paying less or no robux while the game believes you paid more  you pay 2 robux, but the game registers 1000 robux and saves the data as if you donated (or they received) 1000 robux. you boost your stats without paying any robux. using fake donation from the admin panel it doesn&#x27;t increase any stats, so it never appears in the data. "><summary><span class="rb-code">c2</span><span class="rb-rule__main"><span class="rb-rule__title">Using a method to fake donations that appears in data</span><span class="rb-chips"><span Breaking this same rule twice is permanent.</span></span><button type="button" class="rb-copy" data-code="c2"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-link" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.54.54l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7.07-7.07l-1.72 1.71"/><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 7.07l1.71-1.71"/></svg><span>Copy link</span></button></div></div></div></details><details class="rb-rule rb-rule--c" id="c3" data-search="c3 using a method to gain infinite currencies / reputation will get you data wiped found a bug like this? report it to nogo with a **major bug report** ticket on the official discord: you get a reward instead of a ban. using an unintended method to get infinite currency  every time you respawn you get +25 coins straight away, so you keep resetting to farm infinite coins. that obviously isn&#x27;t normal: when caught, you are banned and data wiped. gaining or losing reputation without it costing the other player any currency.   lying in a purchase issue ticket to try to get free currency.   using alts to boost your friend bonus. this isn&#x27;t an unintended method.  staying afk overnight on the lottery. this isn&#x27;t an unintended method. "><summary><span class="rb-code">c3</span><span class="rb-rule__main"><span class="rb-rule__title">Using a method to gain infinite Currencies / Reputation</span><span class="rb-chips"><span 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 7.07l1.71-1.71"/></svg><span>Copy link</span></button></div></div></div></details><details class="rb-rule rb-rule--c is-removed" id="c5" data-search="c5 boosting in ranked ranked is currently removed. boosting used to get your elo wiped and your coins reduced  using alts or friends to boost your own elo in ranked unfairly, without any real fight happening  a player keeps winning against someone who leaves every match straight away, so the matches last less than 30 seconds. nobody would queue ranked again and again only to leave instantly against one specific player, so it&#x27;s boosting. helping someone boost their elo in ranked unfairly, without any real fight happening  a player keeps joining matches and leaving straight away, boosting someone else&#x27;s elo. there&#x27;s no other reason to queue ranked again and again only to leave instantly, so it&#x27;s boosting. beating the same player again and again and winning easily, with real fighting happening with few people in the queue you can easily get the same player many times. as long as there is real fighting, it&#x27;s not boosting. a player beats the same opponent over and over, but the match times show real fights. they might be boosting, or they might just be much better: it can&#x27;t be proven for certain, so it&#x27;s not bannable."><summary><span class="rb-code">c5</span><span class="rb-rule__main"><span class="rb-rule__title">Boosting in Ranked</span><span class="rb-chips"><span class="rb-chip rb-chip--off"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-ban" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><path d="M4.929 4.929 19.07 19.071"/></svg><span>Removed</span></span><span class="rb-chip rb-chip--ex"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" 9 6 6 6-6"/></svg></span></summary><div class="rb-rule__body"><div class="rb-rule__inner"><div class="rb-compare"><div class="rb-side rb-side--ban"><p class="rb-side__head"><svg your own ELO in Ranked unfairly, without any real fight happening</span><span class="rb-sub"><span class="rb-sub__head">For example</span><ul><li><span class="rb-sub__t">A player keeps winning against someone who leaves every match straight away, so the matches last less than 30 seconds.</span> <span class="rb-sub__why">Nobody would queue Ranked again and again only to leave instantly against one specific player, so it&#x27;s boosting.</span></li></ul></span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Helping someone boost their ELO in Ranked unfairly, without any real fight happening</span><span class="rb-sub"><span class="rb-sub__head">For example</span><ul><li><span class="rb-sub__t">A player keeps joining matches and leaving straight away, boosting someone else&#x27;s ELO.</span> <span class="rb-sub__why">There&#x27;s no other reason to queue Ranked again and again only to leave instantly, so it&#x27;s boosting.</span></li></ul></span></li></ul></div><div data-search="c6 bug abuse that can disrupt other players / exploiting / lagging the server  **exploiting** means using &quot;hacks&quot; or unintended third-party software. for admins: lagging the game with something from the admin panel is a strike instead of an instant demotion. flying using exploits, including flying without the fly gamepass mods can tell when a player flies this way, because they hear swimming sound effects.  spectating players in rooms this is impossible unless you are an admin.  walking on air using exploits.   cheating in the arenas using exploits.   attaching yourself to a player using exploits.   using something with collisions to physically move other players.   playing an audio for the whole server, unless it&#x27;s done with the admin command.   using a catalog accessory that makes your character take up a large part of the map here an accessory that&#x27;s far too big has changed the sky of the whole map.  using a bug to make an item take up a large part of the map if an item you&#x27;re holding is bigger than the whole drink area, you are most likely abusing a bug. at size 5 (the biggest size changer size) an item is still smaller than that.  scribbling nonsense in the draw area only to make people lag.   making players lag by spamming an item or activity.   seeing players who are invisible without being an admin.   firing every remote event to try to backdoor the game  you fire every remote event with a small piece of code that tries to create a new object in the game. claiming to sell exploits for the game, on discord or in game.   using exploits only for emotes as long as it doesn&#x27;t break **a3** (simulating nsfw actions with animations). these can sometimes become a viral clip that grows the game.  using size 5 with the size changer gamepass it never makes you big enough to cover a large part of the map, and it&#x27;s an intended feature.  covering a large part of the map because an admin used a size change command on you it wouldn&#x27;t make sense to ban players for something only admins can do.  using a large draw count in the draw area for a real drawing that isn&#x27;t nonsense you are making a real drawing, not drawing only to make people lag.  entering or spectating a room as an admin.  "><summary><span class="rb-code">c6</span><span class="rb-rule__main"><span class="rb-rule__title">Bug Abuse that can disrupt other players / Exploiting / Lagging the server</span><span break <b>a3</b> (simulating NSFW actions with animations). These can sometimes become a viral clip that grows the game.</span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Using Size 5 with the Size Changer gamepass</span><span class="rb-ex__d">It never makes you big enough to cover a large part of the map, and it&#x27;s an intended feature.</span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Covering a large part of the map because an Admin used a Size Change command on you</span><span class="rb-ex__d">It wouldn&#x27;t make sense to ban players for something only Admins can do.</span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Using a large Draw Count in the Draw Area for a real drawing that isn&#x27;t nonsense</span><span class="rb-ex__d">You are making a real drawing, not drawing only to make people lag.</span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Entering or spectating a Room as an Admin.</span></li></ul></div></div><div class="rb-rule__foot"><span class="rb-punish"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-gavel" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m14 13-8.381 8.38a1 1 0 0 1-3.001-3l8.384-8.381M16 16l6-6M21.5 10.5l-8-8M8 8l6-6M8.5 7.5l8 8"/></svg><span>Strike ban: <b>3 days → 7 days → 30 days → permanent</b>. Breaking this same rule twice is permanent.</span></span><button type="button" class="rb-copy" data-code="c6"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-link" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.54.54l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7.07-7.07l-1.72 1.71"/><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 7.07l1.71-1.71"/></svg><span>Copy link</span></button></div></div></div></details><details class="rb-rule rb-rule--c" id="c8" data-search="c8 lying in a player report / misusing ingame player reports   lying on purpose to get another player banned.   creating several (at least 3) fake in-game player reports.   lying on purpose to get someone unbanned they&#x27;re already banned, so there&#x27;s no need to ban them again, and it&#x27;s hard to prove 100% that someone is lying and not just forgetting. mods keep it in mind if they try to appeal again. "><summary><span class="rb-code">c8</span><span class="rb-rule__main"><span class="rb-rule__title">Lying in a Player Report / Misusing Ingame Player Reports</span><span class="rb-chips"><span class="rb-side__head"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-circle-check"
