class="rb-ex__t">Lying on purpose to get someone unbanned</span><span class="rb-ex__d">They&#x27;re already banned, so there&#x27;s no need to ban them again, and it&#x27;s hard to prove 100% that someone is lying and not just forgetting. Mods keep it in mind if they try to appeal again.</span></li></ul></div></div><div class="rb-rule__foot"><span class="rb-punish"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-gavel" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m14 13-8.381 8.38a1 1 0 0 1-3.001-3l8.384-8.381M16 16l6-6M21.5 10.5l-8-8M8 8l6-6M8.5 7.5l8 8"/></svg><span>Strike ban: <b>3 days → 7 days → 30 days → permanent</b>. Breaking this same rule twice is permanent.</span></span><button type="button" class="rb-copy" data-code="c8"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-link" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.54.54l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7.07-7.07l-1.72 1.71"/><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 7.07l1.71-1.71"/></svg><span>Copy link</span></button></div></div></div></details><details class="rb-rule rb-rule--c" id="c9" data-search="c9 creating / showing suggestive content  **suggestive** means content that is intentionally designed to sexualize the body, not content that simply shows skin. that includes drawings with clothing or textures that expose or simulate nudity on genitals, buttocks or nipples (thongs, pasties, lingerie), an exaggerated body shape with minimal clothing that draws sexual focus to those areas, and explicitly sexual animations, poses or accessories. swimwear like bikinis and trunks isn&#x27;t highly suggestive on its own, because it has a clear real-world context. it only counts when combined with the things above. ask yourself: is the drawing designed to look sexual, or does it simply happen to show skin? showing cartoonish depictions of sexual organs  you type a text drawing of a penis or of breasts. you draw 🍆 in a context where you&#x27;re clearly talking about the sexual organ. showing suggestive artwork  a drawing of a woman with breasts of an exaggerated size that looks suggestive. drawing women with breasts is fine on its own, but exaggerated sizes are bannable. showing people or things from nsfw media or explicit content  you build a pole that is clearly meant to be a stripper pole. you draw a character that was originally created in nsfw or explicit media. drawing these characters is bannable whatever the context you draw them in. showing a cropped or edited image of explicit content, even without the explicit parts  you take a picture of a scene from an explicit video and cover all the sexual organs with food. nothing explicit is showing, but it&#x27;s still an edited image of explicit content, so it&#x27;s bannable. you take a picture of a scene from an explicit video and crop it so only the face shows. nothing explicit is showing, but it&#x27;s still a cropped image of explicit content, so it&#x27;s bannable. showing pictures of porn actors, even when there&#x27;s nothing explicit in the picture.   showing places typically used for sex work (strip clubs, buildings with stripper poles).   referring to &quot;👌 👈&quot;.   showing swimsuits without suggestive intent swimsuits don&#x27;t make a drawing suggestive on their own, unless the drawing uses them that way.  showing abs abs show a lot of skin, but they mean the person is fit, not that the drawing is suggestive, unless the drawing uses them in a suggestive way.  showing women with breasts that aren&#x27;t exaggerated in size women have breasts, so drawing them doesn&#x27;t make a drawing suggestive. exaggerated sizes, or clothes meant to be suggestive, would be bannable.  cartoonish depictions of a butt fine when it&#x27;s very cartoonish and obviously not meant to be sexual. "><summary><span class="rb-code">c9</span><span class="rb-rule__main"><span class="rb-rule__title">Creating / Showing suggestive content</span><span class="rb-chips"><span class="rb-chip works</span></p><p><b>Suggestive</b> means content that is intentionally designed to sexualize the body, not content that simply shows skin.</p><p>That includes drawings with clothing or textures that expose or simulate nudity on genitals, buttocks or nipples (thongs, pasties, lingerie), an exaggerated body shape with minimal clothing that draws sexual focus to those areas, and explicitly sexual animations, poses or accessories.</p><p>Swimwear like bikinis and trunks isn&#x27;t highly suggestive on its own, because it has a clear real-world context. <section class="rb-ch rb-ch--group rb-ch--d" id="rb-d" data-title="Permanent bans"><header class="rb-ch__head"><span class="rb-ch__badge rb-letter">d</span><div><span class="rb-ch__kicker">Chapter 5 · ban codes</span><h2 class="rb-ch__title">Permanent bans</h2><p class="rb-ch__about">The most serious rules. Breaking one is a permanent ban straight away.</p></div></header><div class="rb-banner rb-banner--perm"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-octagon-x" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m15 9-6 6M2.586 16.726A2 2 0 0 1 2 15.312V8.688a2 2 0 0 1 .586-1.414l4.688-4.688A2 2 0 0 1 8.688 2h6.624a2 2 0 0 1 1.414.586l4.688 4.688A2 2 0 0 1 22 8.688v6.624a2 2 0 0 1-.586 1.414l-4.688 4.688a2 2 0 0 1-1.414.586H8.688a2 2 0 0 1-1.414-.586zM9 9l6 6"/></svg><span><b>Permanent right away.</b> Some of these count even outside the game.</span></div><div class="rb-rules"><details class="rb-rule rb-rule--d" id="d1" data-search="d1 nsfw imagery  **nsfw imagery** means: hyper-realistic or real-life depictions of sexual organs, images of sex that show sexual organs, and images of real-life gore. if what a player shows isn&#x27;t one of these, the ban is for **c9** (suggestive content) instead. showing nsfw imagery. someone&#x27;s information that isn&#x27;t directly accessible through their roblox / discord  **discord account** means the player&#x27;s linked discord account: the one directly linked to their roblox account, through a verification bot or through the roblox connection on their discord profile. finding a player&#x27;s social media through a connection that isn&#x27;t linked to their roblox or discord account  you check a player&#x27;s roblox profile and there are no social links. you still search their username on google, find one of their social media accounts and ask them &quot;are you [that account]?&quot;. nothing was linked to their roblox or discord account, so you found something they didn&#x27;t want you to find, and it&#x27;s bannable. finding a player&#x27;s social media through a connection linked to their roblox or discord account it would be unfair to ban you for talking about an account they made public. a player has no social links on their roblox profile, but you find their discord account with the same name, and it has a social media account attached. you ask &quot;are you [that account]?&quot;. it was linked to their discord account, so it&#x27;s not bannable. a player&#x27;s roblox profile links to social media x, and through the links on x you find their social media y. you ask &quot;are you [account on y]?&quot;. you found y through links that start from their roblox or discord account, so it&#x27;s not bannable."><summary><span class="rb-code">d3</span><span class="rb-rule__main"><span class="rb-rule__title">Finding someone&#x27;s jeffrey epstein avatar. he is a very well-known pedophile, and wearing a pedophile&#x27;s avatar implies you are one too. you make jokes hinting that you would do explicit things to minors. you show explicit pictures of a minor. being a zoophile, or saying things that hint you are one  you say you are sexually or romantically attracted to animals. you behave inappropriately in ways that involve animals. making rape jokes about yourself.   saying you like minors.   flirting or talking about explicit content with someone you know is a minor, while you are 18+.   adding someone and talking to them off roblox, then sending explicit content unprompted  you add someone on social media after meeting them in game. in your conversations you send explicit pictures or messages that, from the context, they clearly didn&#x27;t want or ask for. this is bannable. taking part in an online community that shares private information or pictures of people or minors.   catfishing to get something from someone, unless you are catching a predator  you make up an identity to get a real relationship with someone. you make up an identity to get real-life money from someone. you make up an identity to get pictures from someone. pretending to be younger than you are to start a friendship or relationship, unless you are catching a predator  you are 20, but tell people you are 18 to befriend people who believe you are that age. you are 20, but put &quot;18&quot; in your bio so people think you are 18. saying you are doing an explicit action in real life because of someone  you tell someone, even with slang or hints, that you are doing something sexual to yourself because of them. you add someone on social media after meeting them in game. in your conversations you tell them you were doing something sexual to yourself while thinking of them, and from the context they clearly didn&#x27;t want or ask for this. this is bannable. threatening to hurt or kill yourself to get something from someone  you tell someone to call you or you&#x27;ll hurt yourself again. you say &quot;i&#x27;ll die right now if you don&#x27;t do x&quot;. using someone&#x27;s explicit pictures as leverage to get something  you say to someone &quot;send me explicit pictures or else i won&#x27;t stop trolling you in game&quot;. scamming someone it&#x27;s predatory by definition, but unless a mod does it (that&#x27;s **e2**), it isn&#x27;t enough for a ban.  catfishing without trying to get something from someone pretending to be someone you&#x27;re not is fine if you don&#x27;t go too far, and if you don&#x27;t pretend to be a mod (that&#x27;s bannable under **c1**). you make up an identity in game and keep it in the game. you pretend to be any gender in game, without taking it outside the game to get something from someone. wearing baby items like a pacifier, or joking about being younger than you are, without using it to start a friendship or relationship it&#x27;s weird, but fine as long as you aren&#x27;t really claiming a younger age to start a friendship or relationship. you pretend to cry. someone asks you &quot;how old are you, you sound 15&quot; and you say &quot;yeah i&#x27;m 15 for sure man&quot; and leave it at that. it&#x27;s a one-off joke: you aren&#x27;t really presenting it as your age to start relationships or friendships. you wear an outfit with a pacifier. not saying your age when someone asks how old you are you are allowed to keep your information private.  you are a pedophile you say you like minors you talk about explicit content to someone you know is a minor while you are 18+ adding someone and talking to them off roblox then sending explicit content unprompted you are participating in an online community that shares private information/pictures of people or minors pretending to be younger than you are with the intention to form a friendship/relationship with someone, unless you are attempting to catch a predator using someone&#x27;s explicit pictures as leverage to get something using self-harm / suicide threats on yourself to get something from someone"><summary><span class="rb-code">d4</span><span class="rb-rule__main"><span someone&#x27;s phone number for prank calls when they agree to it there is consent, so it isn&#x27;t bannable.  saying a random phone number that belongs to no one nobody needs to consent to it, so it isn&#x27;t hurting anyone.  sharing pictures from someone&#x27;s social media that is directly linked to their roblox or discord account if someone made their social media public, it wouldn&#x27;t be <section class="rb-ch rb-ch--group rb-ch--e" id="rb-e" data-title="Account bans"><header class="rb-ch__head"><span class="rb-ch__badge rb-letter">e</span><div><span class="rb-ch__kicker">Chapter 6 · ban codes</span><h2 class="rb-ch__title">Account bans</h2><p class="rb-ch__about">Bans for your account itself: names, scams, alts, age and self-bans.</p></div></header><div class="rb-banner rb-banner--perm"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-octagon-x" viewBox="0 0 class="rb-rules"><details class="rb-rule rb-rule--e" id="e1" data-search="e1 bannable name unbanned once the name is gone from your username and display name you are allowed to play on other accounts section d.   getting around roblox&#x27;s filter for something that isn&#x27;t bannable  your name contains &quot;f*ck&quot;. it gets around roblox&#x27;s filter, but it doesn&#x27;t break any rule of the game, so you aren&#x27;t banned."><summary><span class="rb-code">e1</span><span class="rb-rule__main"><span class="rb-rule__title">Bannable Name</span><span class="rb-chips"><span class="rb-chip "><summary><span class="rb-code">e3</span><span class="rb-rule__main"><span class="rb-rule__title">Ban Evasion</span><span class="rb-chips"><span class="rb-chip rb-chip--perm"><svg 8h.01"/></svg><span>Unbanned only when your original banned account is unbanned.</span></p><div class="rb-explain"><p class="rb-explain__head"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-lightbulb" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M15 14c.2-1 .7-1.7 1.5-2.5 1-.9 1.5-2.2 1.5-3.5A6 6 0 0 0 6 8c0 1 .2 2.2 1.5 3.5.7.7 1.3 1.5 1.5 2.5M9 18h6M10 22h4"/></svg><span>How it works</span></p><p><b>Banned</b> here means banned from this game, not by Roblox and not from voice chat.</p></div><div class="rb-compare"><div class="rb-side rb-side--ban"><p class="rb-side__head"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-circle-x" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><path d="m15 9-6 6M9 9l6 6"/></svg><span>Bannable</span><span class="rb-side__n">3</span></p><ul class="rb-exs"><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Playing on a different account while your main account is banned from the game.</span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Playing on a friend&#x27;s account while your main account is banned.</span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Letting a banned player play on your account.</span></li></ul></div><div class="rb-side rb-side--ok"><p class="rb-side__head"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-circle-check" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><path d="m16 9-5.5 5.5L8 12"/></svg><span>Not bannable</span><span class="rb-side__n">2</span></p><ul class="rb-exs"><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Playing on a different account while your main account is banned from voice chat</span><span class="rb-ex__d">The rule is only for bans from Meet People Across The World.</span></li><li class="rb-ex"><span class="rb-ex__t">Playing on a different account while your main account is banned by Roblox</span><span class="rb-ex__d">The rule is only for bans from Meet People Across The World.</span></li></ul></div></div><div class="rb-related"><p class="rb-related__q"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-circle-help" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><path d="M9.09 9a3 3 0 0 1 5.83 1c0 2-3 3-3 3M12 17h.01"/></svg><span>Why does my Ban Reason show &quot;You created or used an account to avoid an enforcement action taken against another account within this experience&quot;?</span></p><p>This is an automatic ban by Roblox, meaning no one manually banned you. Because alt accounts detection is enabled, roblox may ban you if someone on your account has also played on a banned account. Mods are able to find this &quot;linked&quot; account that got you banned, and then you will have to explain your connection to the account.</p></div><div class="rb-rule__foot"><span class="rb-punish"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-gavel" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m14 13-8.381 8.38a1 1 0 0 1-3.001-3l8.384-8.381M16 16l6-6M21.5 10.5l-8-8M8 8l6-6M8.5 7.5l8 8"/></svg><span>A <b>permanent ban</b> straight away.</span></span><button type="button" class="rb-copy" data-code="e3"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-link" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.54.54l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7.07-7.07l-1.72 1.71"/><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 7.07l1.71-1.71"/></svg><span>Copy link</span></button></div></div></div></details><details class="rb-rule rb-rule--e" id="e4" data-search="e4 self-bans unbanned only when you ask for it through the ticket system  asking for a self-ban through a ticket open a ticket on the official discord and choose &quot;self ban&quot;. the ban only applies to the account linked to your discord account.  asking a mod to ban you in game you can only ask for a self-ban through the ticket system.  asking for a ban on an account that isn&#x27;t linked to your discord account you can only ask for a ban on your linked account. See <b>d4</b> for when pretending to be younger is bannable.</span></li></ul></div></div><div class="rb-rule__foot"><span class="rb-punish"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-gavel" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m14 13-8.381 8.38a1 1 0 0 1-3.001-3l8.384-8.381M16 16l6-6M21.5 10.5l-8-8M8 8l6-6M8.5 7.5l8 8"/></svg><span>A <b>1 year ban</b> instead of a permanent one.</span></span><button type="button" class="rb-copy" data-code="e5"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-link" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.54.54l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7.07-7.07l-1.72 1.71"/><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 7.07l1.71-1.71"/></svg><span>Copy link</span></button></div></div></div></details></div><nav class="rb-turn" aria-label="Turn the page"><a class="rb-turn__btn rb-turn__btn--prev" href="#rb-d"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-arrow-left" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m12 19-7-7 7-7M19 12H5"/></svg><span><small>Previous</small><b>Permanent bans</b></span></a><a class="rb-turn__btn rb-turn__btn--next" href="#rb-not"><span><small>Next</small><b>Not bannable</b></span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-arrow-right" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M5 12h14M12 5l7 7-7 7"/></svg></a></nav><span class="rb-folio">6</span></section>
<section class="rb-ch rb-ch--not " id="rb-not" data-title="Not bannable"><header class="rb-ch__head"><span class="rb-ch__badge rb-ch__badge--icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-circle-check" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><path d="m16 9-5.5 5.5L8 12"/></svg></span><div><span class="rb-ch__kicker">Chapter 7</span><h2 class="rb-ch__title">Not bannable</h2><p class="rb-ch__about">These things are not against the rules, so nobody gets banned for them.</p></div></header><div class="rb-okcards"><div class="rb-okcard"><span class="rb-okcard__icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-circle-check" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><path d="m16 9-5.5 5.5L8 12"/></svg></span><span class="rb-okcard__t">Being very loud or playing Music in VC</span><span class="rb-okcard__d">This on its own does not break any rules.</span></div><div class="rb-okcard"><span class="rb-okcard__icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-circle-check" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><path d="m16 9-5.5 5.5L8 12"/></svg></span><span class="rb-okcard__t">Being annoying or mean without breaking any rules</span><span class="rb-okcard__d">Since they are not breaking rules they wouldn&#x27;t be banned.</span></div><div class="rb-okcard"><span class="rb-okcard__icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-circle-check" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><path d="m16 9-5.5 5.5L8 12"/></svg></span><span class="rb-okcard__t">Not being 18+ in the 18+ version</span><span class="rb-okcard__d">This isn&#x27;t bannable because the unban process would require us to ask for their ID, and it isn&#x27;t our right to request this information. non18+ Players gained access to 18+ because Roblox Verified their account as 18+, and regardless non18+ and 18+ share the same rules.</span></div><div class="rb-okcard"><span class="rb-okcard__icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-circle-check" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><path d="m16 9-5.5 5.5L8 12"/></svg></span><span class="rb-okcard__t">Covering art in the draw area</span><span class="rb-okcard__d">Unless they are also trying to purposefully cause lag, this isn&#x27;t bannable.</span></div><div class="rb-okcard"><span class="rb-okcard__icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-circle-check" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><path d="m16 9-5.5 5.5L8 12"/></svg></span><span class="rb-okcard__t">Someone who follows you through servers</span><span class="rb-okcard__d">This is considered being annoying, instead just Hide &amp; Ignore them.</span></div></div><div class="rb-howto"><p class="rb-howto__head"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-eye-off" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M10.733 5.076a10.744 10.744 0 0 1 11.205 6.575 1 1 0 0 1 0 .696 10.8 10.8 0 0 1-1.444 2.49M14.084 14.158a3 3 0 0 1-4.242-4.242"/><path d="M17.479 17.499a10.75 10.75 0 0 1-15.417-5.151 1 1 0 0 1 0-.696 10.75 10.75 0 0 1 4.446-5.143M2 2l20 20"/></svg><span>Someone is annoying you? Hide them</span></p><ol class="rb-howto__steps"><li>Click on the player, or find them in the <b>Server List</b></li><li>Their <b>Profile Card</b> opens</li><li>Click <b>Hide Player</b></li></ol></div><nav class="rb-turn" aria-label="Turn the page"><a class="rb-turn__btn rb-turn__btn--prev" href="#rb-e"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-arrow-left" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m12 19-7-7 7-7M19 12H5"/></svg><span><small>Previous</small><b>Account bans</b></span></a><a class="rb-turn__btn rb-turn__btn--next" href="#rb-unbans"><span><small>Next</small><b>Unbans</b></span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-arrow-right" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M5 12h14M12 5l7 7-7 7"/></svg></a></nav><span class="rb-folio">7</span></section>
<section class="rb-ch rb-ch--unbans " id="rb-unbans" data-title="Unbans"><header class="rb-ch__head"><span class="rb-ch__badge rb-ch__badge--icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-unlock" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><rect width="18" height="11" x="3" y="11" rx="2" ry="2"/><path d="M7 11V7a5 5 0 0 1 9.9-1"/></svg></span><div><span class="rb-ch__kicker">Chapter 8</span><h2 class="rb-ch__title">Unbans</h2><p class="rb-ch__about">You can ask for the proof of your ban, and many bans can end early.</p></div></header><div class="rb-usteps"><div class="rb-ustep"><span class="rb-ustep__icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-file-search" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M6 22a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V4a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h8a2.4 2.4 0 0 1 1.704.706l3.588 3.588A2.4 2.4 0 0 1 20 8v12a2 2 0 0 1-2 2z"/><path d="M14 2v5a1 1 0 0 0 1 1h5"/><circle cx="11.5" cy="14.5" r="2.5"/><path d="M13.3 16.3 15 18"/></svg></span><span>You are always allowed to request the proof that got you banned, but you can&#x27;t request who banned you.</span></div><div class="rb-ustep"><span class="rb-ustep__icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-clock-3" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><path d="M12 6v6h4"/></svg></span><span>You can be unbanned early by any mod if they believe you won&#x27;t break the rule again (with the exceptions below).</span></div><div class="rb-ustep"><span class="rb-ustep__icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-hand-heart" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M11 14h2a2 2 0 0 0 0-4h-3c-.6 0-1.1.2-1.4.6L3 16"/><path d="m14.45 13.39 5.05-4.694C20.196 8 21 6.85 21 5.75a2.75 2.75 0 0 0-4.797-1.837.276.276 0 0 1-.406 0A2.75 2.75 0 0 0 11 5.75c0 1.2.802 2.248 1.5 2.946L16 11.95M2 15l6 6"/><path d="m7 20 1.6-1.4c.3-.4.8-.6 1.4-.6h4c1.1 0 2.1-.4 2.8-1.2l4.6-4.4a1 1 0 0 0-2.75-2.91"/></svg></span><span>If you were banned for doing something to another user, you can also be unbanned if they wish for you to be unbanned.</span></div><div class="rb-ustep"><span class="rb-ustep__icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-scale" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 3v18M19 8l3 8a5 5 0 0 1-6 0zV7"/><path d="M3 7h1a17 17 0 0 0 8-2 17 17 0 0 0 8 2h1M5 8l3 8a5 5 0 0 1-6 0zV7M7 21h10"/></svg></span><span>If you believe you were unfairly banned, the best way to prove your innocence is to find the matching Ban Code (x#) that banned you, and explain to the mod why your Ban Proof did not violate anything found in the Bannable Examples in the matching Ban Code Post.</span></div></div><div class="rb-banner rb-banner--info"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-scale" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 3v18M19 8l3 8a5 5 0 0 1-6 0zV7"/><path d="M3 7h1a17 17 0 0 0 8-2 17 17 0 0 0 8 2h1M5 8l3 8a5 5 0 0 1-6 0zV7M7 21h10"/></svg><span><b>A ban only counts</b> if what you did matches one of the bannable examples of its ban code.</span></div><div class="rb-nevers"><p class="rb-nevers__head"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-shield-x" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M20 13c0 5-3.5 7.5-7.66 8.95a1 1 0 0 1-.67-.01C7.5 20.5 4 18 4 13V6a1 1 0 0 1 1-1c2 0 4.5-1.2 6.24-2.72a1.17 1.17 0 0 1 1.52 0C14.51 3.81 17 5 19 5a1 1 0 0 1 1 1zM14.5 9.5l-5 5M9.5 9.5l5 5"/></svg><span>Never unbanned</span></p><p>You can&#x27;t be unbanned <b>ever</b> for the following examples, and you can be banned for these even if it takes place outside of the game (unless your ban is verifiably false):</p><div class="rb-never-list"><a class="rb-never" href="#d2"><span class="rb-code rb-code--sm">d2</span><span>Threatening a Player / Server</span></a><a class="rb-never" href="#d4"><span class="rb-code rb-code--sm">d4</span><span>Predatory Behavior</span></a><a class="rb-never" href="#d5"><span class="rb-code rb-code--sm">d5</span><span>Offering/Giving something in exchange for explicit pictures</span></a><a class="rb-never" href="#d6"><span class="rb-code rb-code--sm">d6</span><span>Actively trying to make someone harm themselves</span></a><a class="rb-never" href="#d7"><span class="rb-code rb-code--sm">d7</span><span>Giving private information about someone without their consent</span></a></div><p class="rb-nevers__tip">Open each rule to see exactly which cases are never unbanned.</p></div><nav class="rb-turn" aria-label="Turn the page"><a class="rb-turn__btn rb-turn__btn--prev" href="#rb-not"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-arrow-left" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m12 19-7-7 7-7M19 12H5"/></svg><span><small>Previous</small><b>Not bannable</b></span></a><a class="rb-turn__btn rb-turn__btn--next" href="#rb-faq"><span><small>Next</small><b>Questions</b></span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-arrow-right" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M5 12h14M12 5l7 7-7 7"/></svg></a></nav><span class="rb-folio">8</span></section>
<section class="rb-ch rb-ch--faq " id="rb-faq" data-title="Questions"><header class="rb-ch__head"><span class="rb-ch__badge rb-ch__badge--icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-circle-help" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><path d="M9.09 9a3 3 0 0 1 5.83 1c0 2-3 3-3 3M12 17h.01"/></svg></span><div><span class="rb-ch__kicker">Chapter 9</span><h2 class="rb-ch__title">Questions</h2><p class="rb-ch__about">Answers from the moderators to the questions they get the most.</p></div></header><div class="rb-faqs"><details class="rb-faq" id="faq-why-does-my-ban-reason-show-you-created-or-used-" data-search="why does my ban reason show &quot;you created or used an account to avoid an enforcement action taken against another account within this experience&quot;? this is an automatic ban by roblox, meaning no one manually banned you. because alt accounts detection is enabled, roblox may ban you if someone on your account has also played on a banned account. mods are able to find this &quot;linked&quot; account that got you banned, and then you will have to explain your connection to the account."><summary><span class="rb-faq__q">?</span><span class="rb-faq__t">Why does my Ban Reason show &quot;You created or used an account to avoid an enforcement action taken against another account within this experience&quot;?</span><span class="rb-rule__toggle" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-chevron-down" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"/></svg></span></summary><div class="rb-rule__body"><div class="rb-rule__inner"><p class="rb-faq__a">This is an automatic ban by Roblox, meaning no one manually banned you. Because alt accounts detection is enabled, roblox may ban you if someone on your account has also played on a banned account. Mods are able to find this &quot;linked&quot; account that got you banned, and then you will have to explain your connection to the account.</p></div></div></details><details class="rb-faq" id="faq-why-did-only-i-get-banned-and-not-them" data-search="why did only i get banned and not them? bans are fully manual, so you are only banned if caught. you were banned because you were reported by a player or seen by a mod. if you never report a player, they may never get banned."><summary><span class="rb-faq__q">?</span><span class="rb-faq__t">Why did only I get banned and not them?</span><span class="rb-rule__toggle" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-chevron-down" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"/></svg></span></summary><div class="rb-rule__body"><div class="rb-rule__inner"><p class="rb-faq__a">Bans are fully manual, so you are only banned if caught. You were banned because you were reported by a player or seen by a mod. If you never report a player, they may never get banned.</p></div></div></details><details class="rb-faq" id="faq-why-is-my-ticket-taking-so-long-to-get-answered" data-search="why is my ticket taking so long to get answered? mod work is purely voluntarily, as there is no payment involved from answering tickets. a mod will get to your ticket when they aren&#x27;t busy, but they do not owe you their time."><summary><span class="rb-faq__q">?</span><span class="rb-faq__t">Why is my ticket taking so long to get answered?</span><span class="rb-rule__toggle" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-chevron-down" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"/></svg></span></summary><div class="rb-rule__body"><div class="rb-rule__inner"><p class="rb-faq__a">Mod work is purely voluntarily, as there is no payment involved from answering tickets. A Mod will get to your ticket when they aren&#x27;t busy, but they do not owe you their time.</p></div></div></details><details class="rb-faq" id="faq-i-feel-like-a-mod-abused-their-power-what-do-i-d" data-search="i feel like a mod abused their power, what do i do? instead of a player report, submit an admin report. you can view all the mod rules on the community discord."><summary><span class="rb-faq__q">?</span><span class="rb-faq__t">I feel like a Mod abused their power, what do I do?</span><span class="rb-rule__toggle" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-chevron-down" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"/></svg></span></summary><div class="rb-rule__body"><div class="rb-rule__inner"><p class="rb-faq__a">Instead of a Player Report, submit an Admin Report. You can view all the Mod Rules on the community Discord.</p></div></div></details><details class="rb-faq" id="faq-why-are-some-ban-codes-empty" data-search="why are some ban codes empty? rules can change over time, and some rules are phased out or merged, which can leave some ban codes empty."><summary><span class="rb-faq__q">?</span><span class="rb-faq__t">Why are some Ban Codes empty?</span><span class="rb-rule__toggle" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-chevron-down" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"/></svg></span></summary><div class="rb-rule__body"><div class="rb-rule__inner"><p class="rb-faq__a">Rules can change over time, and some rules are phased out or merged, which can leave some Ban Codes empty.</p></div></div></details><details class="rb-faq" id="faq-i-want-a-rule-changed" data-search="i want a rule changed. you can leave suggestions for rule changes on the community discord."><summary><span class="rb-faq__q">?</span><span class="rb-faq__t">I want a Rule Changed.</span><span class="rb-rule__toggle" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-chevron-down" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"/></svg></span></summary><div class="rb-rule__body"><div class="rb-rule__inner"><p class="rb-faq__a">You can leave suggestions for Rule Changes on the community Discord.</p></div></div></details><details class="rb-faq" id="faq-will-i-get-in-trouble-if-i-don-t-report-someone-" data-search="will i get in trouble if i don&#x27;t report someone who breaks rules? no, players don&#x27;t have to report players if they don&#x27;t want to."><summary><span class="rb-faq__q">?</span><span class="rb-faq__t">Will I get in trouble if I don&#x27;t report someone who breaks rules?</span><span class="rb-rule__toggle" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-chevron-down" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"/></svg></span></summary><div class="rb-rule__body"><div class="rb-rule__inner"><p class="rb-faq__a">No, players don&#x27;t have to report players if they don&#x27;t want to.</p></div></div></details><details class="rb-faq" id="faq-i-saw-a-mod-see-someone-break-rules-and-they-did" data-search="i saw a mod see someone break rules and they didn&#x27;t ban them! mods don&#x27;t have to ban players if they don&#x27;t want to, they can instead choose to warn them or ignore them instead."><summary><span class="rb-faq__q">?</span><span class="rb-faq__t">I saw a Mod see someone break rules and they didn&#x27;t ban them!</span><span class="rb-rule__toggle" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-chevron-down" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"/></svg></span></summary><div class="rb-rule__body"><div class="rb-rule__inner"><p class="rb-faq__a">Mods don&#x27;t have to ban players if they don&#x27;t want to, they can instead choose to warn them or ignore them instead.</p></div></div></details><details class="rb-faq" id="faq-what-punishments-do-mods-get-if-they-break-rules" data-search="what punishments do mods get if they break rules? mods breaking any of the game rules will lose their mod and be banned. mods breaking the mod rules will receive a mod strike instead, and at 3 lose their mod."><summary><span class="rb-faq__q">?</span><span class="rb-faq__t">What punishments do Mods get if they break rules?</span><span class="rb-rule__toggle" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-chevron-down" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"/></svg></span></summary><div class="rb-rule__body"><div class="rb-rule__inner"><p class="rb-faq__a">Mods breaking any of the game rules will lose their mod and be banned. Mods breaking the Mod Rules will receive a Mod Strike instead, and at 3 lose their Mod.</p></div></div></details><details class="rb-faq" id="faq-i-want-to-be-mod" data-search="i want to be mod! mod applications open from time to time. the requirements are playing the game a lot, and passing the rules test, and some luck too."><summary><span class="rb-faq__q">?</span><span class="rb-faq__t">I want to be Mod!</span><span class="rb-rule__toggle" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-chevron-down" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"/></svg></span></summary><div class="rb-rule__body"><div class="rb-rule__inner"><p class="rb-faq__a">Mod Applications open from time to time. The requirements are playing the game a lot, and passing the rules test, and some luck too.</p></div></div></details><details class="rb-faq" id="faq-who-decided-these-rules" data-search="who decided these rules? the rules are based on what the owner doesn&#x27;t want ingame."><summary><span class="rb-faq__q">?</span><span class="rb-faq__t">Who decided these rules?</span><span class="rb-rule__toggle" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-chevron-down" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"/></svg></span></summary><div class="rb-rule__body"><div class="rb-rule__inner"><p class="rb-faq__a">The rules are based on what the owner doesn&#x27;t want ingame.</p></div></div></details><details class="rb-faq" id="faq-how-do-build-related-rules-work" data-search="how do build related rules work? players who collaborate on a build that contains bannable content will be banned, but only if they were aware of it. if a player joins a public build and secretly creates bannable content without others noticing, the other builders in that space are not held responsible. banning someone requires that they knowingly allowed it to happen. however, if a player witnesses another builder creating bannable content and continues to build alongside them without reporting or stopping it, they are considered a willing participant and will be banned for the same reason as the offending player."><summary><span class="rb-faq__q">?</span><span class="rb-faq__t">How do Build Related Rules work?</span><span class="rb-rule__toggle" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-chevron-down" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"/></svg></span></summary><div class="rb-rule__body"><div class="rb-rule__inner"><p class="rb-faq__a">Players who collaborate on a build that contains bannable content will be banned, but only if they were aware of it. If a player joins a public build and secretly creates bannable content without others noticing, the other builders in that space are not held responsible. Banning someone requires that they knowingly allowed it to happen. However, if a player witnesses another builder creating bannable content and continues to build alongside them without reporting or stopping it, they are considered a willing participant and will be banned for the same reason as the offending player.</p></div></div></details><details class="rb-faq" id="faq-can-i-be-banned-for-stuff-that-happens-outside-t" data-search="can i be banned for stuff that happens outside the game? no. breaking any of the rules outside the game, such as a separate discord, will not get you banned in game. bans can only be linked if you break the rule in the community discord (discord.gg/vcworld) or you break one of the &quot;unbannables&quot;."><summary><span class="rb-faq__q">?</span><span class="rb-faq__t">Can I be banned for stuff that happens outside the game?</span><span class="rb-rule__toggle" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-chevron-down" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"/></svg></span></summary><div class="rb-rule__body"><div class="rb-rule__inner"><p class="rb-faq__a">No. Breaking any of the rules outside the game, such as a separate Discord, will not get you banned in game. Bans can only be linked if you break the rule in the community Discord (discord.gg/vcworld) or you break one of the &quot;Unbannables&quot;.</p></div></div></details><details class="rb-faq" id="faq-can-i-make-unban-requests-for-my-friend-alt" data-search="can i make unban requests for my friend / alt? unless your friend is banned from the discord, they must make the unban request on an account verified with the same username. if it&#x27;s your alt account, you must prove ownership."><summary><span class="rb-faq__q">?</span><span class="rb-faq__t">Can I make Unban Requests for my friend / alt?</span><span class="rb-rule__toggle" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-chevron-down" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"/></svg></span></summary><div class="rb-rule__body"><div class="rb-rule__inner"><p class="rb-faq__a">Unless your friend is Banned from the Discord, they must make the Unban Request on an account Verified with the same Username. If it&#x27;s your Alt Account, you must prove ownership.</p></div></div></details><details class="rb-faq" id="faq-can-i-be-banned-for-signing-a-clipboard" data-search="can i be banned for signing a clipboard? no. if a clipboard is bannable, only the owner of the clipboard would be banned, not any of the signers."><summary><span class="rb-faq__q">?</span><span class="rb-faq__t">Can I be banned for signing a clipboard?</span><span class="rb-rule__toggle" aria-hidden="true"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-chevron-down" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"/></svg></span></summary><div class="rb-rule__body"><div class="rb-rule__inner"><p class="rb-faq__a">No. If a Clipboard is bannable, only the Owner of the clipboard would be banned, not any of the signers.</p></div></div></details></div><nav class="rb-turn" aria-label="Turn the page"><a class="rb-turn__btn rb-turn__btn--prev" href="#rb-unbans"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-arrow-left" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m12 19-7-7 7-7M19 12H5"/></svg><span><small>Previous</small><b>Unbans</b></span></a><a class="rb-turn__btn rb-turn__btn--next" href="#rb-discord"><span><small>Next</small><b>Discord bans</b></span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-arrow-right" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M5 12h14M12 5l7 7-7 7"/></svg></a></nav><span class="rb-folio">9</span></section>
<section class="rb-ch rb-ch--discord " id="rb-discord" data-title="Discord bans"><header class="rb-ch__head"><span class="rb-ch__badge rb-ch__badge--icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-message-circle" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M2.992 16.342a2 2 0 0 1 .094 1.167l-1.065 3.29a1 1 0 0 0 1.236 1.168l3.413-.998a2 2 0 0 1 1.099.092 10 10 0 1 0-4.777-4.719"/></svg></span><div><span class="rb-ch__kicker">Chapter 10</span><h2 class="rb-ch__title">Discord bans</h2><p class="rb-ch__about">How bans work on the community Discord server.</p></div></header><ul class="rb-list"><li>Instead of being banned from the Discord, you will be given a special role (with some exceptions).</li><li>Exceptions: anything under the &quot;Unbannables&quot;, being under 13, or using an alt account to bypass those bans. If any of those 3 scenarios are met then you will be banned from the Discord instead.</li><li>Anyone who was banned from the Discord previously who doesn&#x27;t meet the exceptions above can be unbanned.</li></ul><nav class="rb-turn" aria-label="Turn the page"><a class="rb-turn__btn rb-turn__btn--prev" href="#rb-faq"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" class="lucide lucide-arrow-left" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m12 19-7-7 7-7M19 12H5"/></svg><span><small>Previous</small><b>Questions</b></span></a><span></span></nav><span class="rb-folio">10</span></section>
